# D-9 set/tag membership conversion

Generated by
`uv run --no-project scripts/rebuild_convert_sets_tags.py --write-curation`.

Source inventory: 48 explicit sets, 648 set memberships; 6 tags, 24 variable memberships
and 12 register memberships. Exact current local ledger ids are the conversion
authority. Missing ids are refused for D-12; delivery-column hints never supply
identity. Existing membership setters are consolidated per variable and field. Unrelated
authored statements and all locks are preserved.

This is static source accounting. Lock, curated build and verify were not run; placement
and final resolver selection remain unverified. Tag search is not implemented.
Presentation fields are dropped with reasons below; register memberships remain
deferred.

Sets with fewer than two distinct locally pinned members after refusals are deferred;
their remaining memberships are not emitted. A local ledger id does not by itself prove
final placement.

  | Field         | Converted memberships | Refused memberships | Deferred memberships |
  | ------------- | --------------------: | ------------------: | -------------------: |
  | sets          |                   534 |                 113 |                    1 |
  | tags          |                    22 |                   2 |                    0 |
  | register tags |                     0 |                   0 |                   12 |
